F . Halon Fire Extinguisher Check
SUBTASK 26-26-01-200-009
(1) Use force gauge, COM-373, to weigh the fire extinguisher.
(2) Compare the value against the weight shown on the applicable decal, nameplate, or label
located on the fire extinguisher.
(a) If the weight of the fire extinguisher has decreased by more than 0.125 lb (0.057 kg) from
the weight recorded on the applicable decal, nameplate, or label, the fire extinguisher has
a leak, replace the fire extinguisher.
(b) Make sure that the weight of the fire extinguisher is 3.5 lb (1.59 kg) - 3.81 lb (1.73 kg).
1) If the weight of the fire extinguisher is not correct, replace the fire extinguisher.
NOTE: A complete fire extinguisher includes the pull-out pin, pull-tite seal, hose
assembly with hose strap, or nozzle, as applicable.

nameplate, or label.

(3) Make sure that the handle lockpin [2] and pull-tite seal [1] are in their correct positions on the
handle.
(a) If the pull-tite seal [1] is missing or broken, replace the pull-tite seal [1] or fire extinguisher
bottle.
SUBTASK 26-26-01-200-011
(4) Examine the pressure gauge.
(a) Make sure that the fire extinguisher has the correct pressure.

26-26-02-200-801Water Fire Extinguishers - Inspection/CheckPDF p. 755
Open original AMM PDF - page 755 ↗ 
2. Water Fire Extinguishers - Inspection/Check
(Figure 601)
A. General
(1) The portable water fire extinguishers are installed in several general locations in the passenger
compartment. If the water fire extinguisher is not easy to see, a placard will identify the
location. The water fire extinguishers are usually installed in one or more of these areas: galley
or lavatory stowage areas, closets, doghouses, near attendant seats.
(2) This task has a weight check of the portable water fire extinguisher assembly and the weight
check of the CO
2 cartridge.

G. Water Fire Extinguishers Inspection
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-001
(1) Make sure that the instruction decal and the nameplate are in good condition.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-010
(2) Do this task: Water Fire Extinguisher Removal, TASK 26-26-02-000-801.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-006
(3) Make sure that there is no damage to the water fire extinguisher.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-008
(4) Make sure that there are no leaks in the water fire extinguisher.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-009
(5) If there are other manufacturer inspection or maintenance procedures that show on the water
fire extinguisher, do those procedures.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-004
(6) Make sure that the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is correctly installed on the handle.
(a) If the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is missing or broken, do one of the following
steps:
1) Replace the water fire extinguisher.
2) Weigh the water fire extinguisher and CO
2 cartridge [2].
a) Do the step to weigh the water fire extinguisher.
26-26-02
b) Do the steps to make sure that the water fire extinguisher is not pressurized
and to remove and weigh the CO 2 cartridge [2].
c) If the weight of the water fire extinguisher and the CO 2 cartridge [2] are good,
do the step to assemble the cartridge holder [5].
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-011
(7) Do a weight check of the portable water fire extinguisher assembly.
(a) Use a force gauge, COM-11525, push/pull scale 0-25 pound (0-11 kilogram), STD-753, or
equivalent to make sure that the weight of the water fire extinguisher is not less than the
weight shown on the extinguisher nameplate.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-019
(8) Make sure that the water fire extinguisher is not pressurized.
WARNING
BEFORE YOU DISASSEMBLE THE EXTINGUISHER, MAKE SURE THAT
YOU REMOVE ALL THE PRESSURE FROM THE FIRE EXTINGUISHER.
WHEN YOU DISASSEMBLE A PRESSURIZED FIRE EXTINGUISHER, IT
CAN CAUSE INJURY .
(a) Do a visual check of the fire extinguisher.
1) Make sure that the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is not broken or missing.
2) Make sure that the red line on the valve assembly and the red line on the cartridge
holder [5] are aligned.
(b) If the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is broken, missing, or the cartridge holder [5]
alignment is not correct, do these steps:
1) Push and hold the valve assembly lever down.
a) If there is air pressure and/or extinguishing agent released at the nozzle,
replace the water fire extinguisher.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-020-005
(9) Remove the CO
2 cartridge [2].
(a) Remove and discard the lockwire seal [1].
(b) Turn the cartridge holder [5] counterclockwise and remove it from the valve assembly.
(c) Remove the spring [4] and CO
2 cartridge [2] from the cartridge holder [5].
1) Discard the spring [4].
2) If the CO
2 cartridge [2] is damaged or used, replace the CO 2 cartridge [2].
(d) Remove the O-ring [3] from the valve assembly.
1) Discard the O-ring [3].
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-020
(10) Do a weight check of the CO 2 cartridge [2].
(a) Put the CO 2 cartridge [2] on a scale (2 lb force gauge, COM-16140, or equivalent).
(b) Make sure that the CO 2 cartridge [2] weighs a minimum of 0.073 lb (33 g).
1) If the CO 2 cartridge [2] weighs less than 0.073 lb (33 g), discard the CO 2
cartridge [2] and install a new CO 2 cartridge [2].
SUBTASK 26-26-02-420-004
(11) Assemble the cartridge holder [5].
(a) Put a thin layer of lubricant, D00062, on the new O-ring [3].
26-26-02
(b) Install the O-ring [3] in the valve assembly.
(c) Put a thin layer of grease, D00013, on the internal threads of the cartridge holder [5].
(d) Install the new spring [4] and CO 2 cartridge [2] in the cartridge holder [5].
(e) Install the cartridge holder [5] in the valve assembly and turn the cartridge holder [5]
clockwise until:
1) The red line on the cartridge holder [5] aligns with the red line on the valve
assembly.
2) The lockwire seal [1] holes in the lock ring [6] and valve assembly are aligned.
a) If it is necessary, loosen the setscrews [7] to adjust the position of the lock
ring [6].
(f) Install a new lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) through the holes in the lock ring [6]
and the body of the valve assembly.
(g) Install the wires through the lead seal.
1) Pull the wires tight and crimp the lead seal with the crimping tool, STD-252.
NOTE: It is satisfactory to use a standard plier to crimp the lead seal.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-002
(12) Make sure that the mounting bracket is attached correctly to the airplane.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-012
(13) Do this task: Water Fire Extinguisher Installation, TASK 26-26-02-400-801.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-210-003
(14) Make sure that the water fire extinguisher is installed tightly to the mounting bracket.

G. Prepare for the Operational Test
SUBTASK 26-26-02-010-003
(1) Do this task: Water Fire Extinguisher Removal, TASK 26-26-02-000-801.
26-26-02
SUBTASK 26-26-02-710-001
(2) Make sure that the water fire extinguisher is not pressurized.
WARNING
BEFORE YOU DISASSEMBLE THE EXTINGUISHER, MAKE SURE THAT
YOU REMOVE ALL THE PRESSURE FROM THE FIRE EXTINGUISHER.
WHEN YOU DISASSEMBLE A PRESSURIZED FIRE EXTINGUISHER, IT
CAN CAUSE INJURY .
(a) Visually check the water fire extinguisher.
1) Make sure that the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is not broken or missing.
2) Make sure that the red line on the valve assembly and the red line on the cartridge
holder [5] are visible and aligned.
(b) If the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is broken, missing, or the cartridge holder [5]
alignment is not correct, do the following steps:
1) Push and hold the valve assembly lever down until all the pressure and
extinguishing agent are removed.
a) If there is air pressure and or extinguishing agent discharging at the nozzle,
replace the water fire extinguisher.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-710-002
(3) Remove the CO
2 cartridge [2].
(a) Remove and discard the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248).
(b) Turn the cartridge holder [5] counterclockwise and remove it from the valve assembly.
(c) Remove the spring [4] and CO
2 cartridge [2] from the cartridge holder [5].
1) Discard the spring [4].
2) If the CO
2 cartridge [2] is damaged or used, replace the CO 2 cartridge [2].
(d) Remove the O-ring [3] from the valve assembly.
1) Discard the O-ring [3].
H. CO 2 Cartridge Weight Check
SUBTASK 26-26-02-710-003
(1) Check the weight of the CO 2 cartridge [2].
(a) Put the CO 2 cartridge [2] on a scale (2 lb force gauge, COM-16140, or equivalent).
(b) Make sure that the CO 2 cartridge [2] weighs a minimum of 0.073 lb (33 g).
1) If the CO 2 cartridge [2] weighs less than 0.073 lb (33 g), discard and install a new
CO2 cartridge [2].
I. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition
SUBTASK 26-26-02-710-004
(1) Assemble the cartridge holder [5].
(a) Put a thin layer of lubricant, D00062, on the new O-ring [3].
(b) Install the O-ring [3] in the valve assembly.
(c) Put a thin layer of grease, D00013, on the internal threads of the cartridge holder [5].
(d) Install the new spring [4] and CO
2 cartridge [2] in the cartridge holder [5].
(e) Install the cartridge holder [5] in the valve assembly.
26-26-02
(f) Turn the cartridge holder [5] clockwise until the red line on the cartridge holder [5] aligns
with the red line on the valve assembly.
(g) Make sure that the hole of the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) in the lock ring [6]
aligns with the hole in the valve assembly.
1) If the holes are not aligned, loosen the setscrews [7] and adjust the position of the
lock ring [6].
(h) Install a new lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) through the holes in the lock ring [6]
and the body of the valve assembly.
(i) Install the wires through the lead seal.
1) Pull the wires tight and crimp the lead seal with a crimping tool, STD-252.
NOTE: It is satisfactory to use a standard plier to crimp the lead seal.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-410-006
(2) Do this task: Water Fire Extinguisher Installation, TASK 26-26-02-400-801.

G. Water-type Fire Extinguisher Check
SUBTASK 26-26-02-200-002
(1) Make sure that the lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is correctly installed on the handle.
(a) If the Lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) is missing or broken, or the alignment of the
red line on the cartridge holder is not correct, do one of the following steps:
1) Replace the water fire extinguisher.
26-26-02
2) Weigh the water fire extinguisher and CO 2 cartridge [2].
a) Do the step to weigh the water fire extinguisher.
b) Make sure that the water fire extinguisher is not pressurized and remove and
weigh the CO
2 cartridge [2].
<1> Push and hold the valve assembly lever down.
<a> If there is air pressure and/or extinguishing agent discharging at
the nozzle, replace the fire extinguisher.
c) If the weight of the water fire extinguisher and the CO 2 cartridge [2] are good,
do the step to assemble the cartridge holder [5].
SUBTASK 26-26-02-200-003
(2) Do this step to weigh the water fire extinguisher:
(a) Use the force gauge, COM-11525, push/pull scale 0-25 pound (0-11 kilogram), STD-753,
or equivalent to make sure that the weight of the water fire extinguisher is not less than
the weight shown on the fire extinguisher nameplate.
SUBTASK 26-26-02-200-004
(3) Remove the CO
2 cartridge [2].
(a) Remove and discard the lockwire seal [1].
(b) Turn the cartridge holder [5] counterclockwise and remove it from the valve assembly.
(c) Remove the spring [4] and CO
2 cartridge [2]from the cartridge holder [5].
1) If the CO 2 cartridge [2] is damaged or used, replace the CO 2 cartridge [2].
(d) Remove the O-ring [3] from the valve assembly.
1) Discard the O-ring [3].
SUBTASK 26-26-02-200-005
(4) Check the weight of the CO 2 cartridge [2].
(a) Put the CO 2 cartridge [2] on a scale (2 lb force gauge, COM-16140, or equivalent).
(b) Make sure that the CO 2 cartridge [2] weighs a minimum of 0.073 lb (33 g).
1) If the CO 2 cartridge [2] weighs less than 0.073 lb (33 g), do the following steps:
a) Discard the CO 2 cartridge [2]
b) Install a new CO 2 cartridge [2]
SUBTASK 26-26-02-200-006
(5) Assemble the cartridge holder [5].
(a) Put a thin layer of lubricant, D00062, on the new O-ring [3].
(b) Install the new O-ring [3] in the valve assembly.
1) Use O-ring, packing, D50354.
(c) Put a thin layer of grease, D00013, on the internal threads of the cartridge holder [5].
(d) Install the spring [4] and CO
2 cartridge [2] in the cartridge holder [5].
(e) Install the cartridge holder [5] in the valve assembly and turn the cartridge holder [5]
clockwise until:
1) The red line on the cartridge holder [5] aligns with the red line on the valve
assembly.
2) The lockwire seal [1] holes in the lock ring [6] and valve assembly are aligned.
26-26-02
a) If it is necessary, loosen the setscrew [7] to adjust the position of the lock
ring [6].
(f) Install a new lockwire seal [1] (seal wire, G51248) through the holes in the lock ring [6]
and the body of the valve assembly.
(g) Install the wires through the lead seal.
1) Pull the wires tight and crimp the lead seal with crimping tool, STD-252.
NOTE: It is satisfactory to use a standard plier to crimp the lead seal.
